([Crawlora-org/crawlora-skills](https://github.com/Crawlora-org/crawlora-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/music-podcast-research) · ★1)
- **music-pr-campaign**: Create music PR campaigns with media outreach. TRIGGERS - Use when user needs help with music-pr-campaign related tasks. ([lionelsimai/claude-skills-collection](https://github.com/lionelsimai/claude-skills-collection/tree/HEAD/skills/music-pr-campaign) · ★39 · 3 copies)
- **music-practice**: Arma un plan de práctica musical por sesiones con calentamiento, respiración, vocalización o técnica del instrumento, repertorio con partituras y un registro de avance, sin prometer resultados. Úsala con «hazme un plan para practicar canto», «quiero practicar guitarra 20 minutos al día», «cómo calentar la voz antes del coro». ([anndreloopez012/pack-skills-agents-campuslands-ia-0](https://github.com/anndreloopez012/pack-skills-agents-campuslands-ia-0/tree/HEAD/skills/music-practice))
- **music-practice-coach**: Deliberate practice methodology, scheduling systems, plateau-breaking strategies, motivation techniques, and progress tracking for musicians at any level and on any instrument Use when the user asks about music practice coach, related techniques, best practices, or needs guidance in this domain. Do NOT use when the request is outside the scope of music practice coach or requires a different specialized skill. ([FerroxLabs/wayland](https://github.com/FerroxLabs/wayland/tree/HEAD/src/process/resources/skills-library/bodies/skills/creative-arts/music-practice-coach) · ★618 · 3 copies)
